PID regional buses · Kladensko (okres Kladno) · Monday 2 Nov 2026

PID Kladensko

Every PID bus line of okres Kladno (licences 22xxxx) and the Prague and other lines whose outer end lies here, set as Simutrans lines with the owner's regional method: each line read over the whole day at its core points, a repeating interval, destination and shift variants on their own longer intervals (at most 480 min), timetables at the terminals worked back from the knots, every afternoon stop on a line, buses sized from the pattern. Kladensko: okres Kladno with Slánsko, the Prague lines whose outer end is here and the district lines from Kladno, Slaný and Velvary. Service day Mon 2 Nov 2026. Every terminus is typed as a loop (1 min early); the real minutes are shown. The Prague lines are outstation lines (their buses sleep in Kladno, Slaný, Libušín, Unhošť): the afternoon sends more buses out of Prague than come back, so each runs at the rate of its trips towards Prague and the extra outbound departures are listed as buses going home. Short workings with no stop of their own get their own Simutrans line where they run at least three times each way 13:00 – 19:00 and regularly in the model hours (306 Hostivice, 306 Jeneč, 307 and 386 Unhošť, 342 Knovíz, 350 Velké Přílepy, 365 Unhošť, 389 Slaný, 389 Chomutov, 618 Stochov, 624 Kladno, 626 Theodor; read at the stop where they turn, counting every trip that passes it, as the 3 Nov re-check asks); the others are listed on their card. Three rows keep a 0-minute turn of the real timetable (587 Byseň at Tuřany, Byseň; 588 at Milý, Bor; 627 at Slaný): typed 1 min early as every loop, the bus leaves within the entry's automatic Leave up to. Through running seen in the timetable (0 – 5 min at the terminal; the 2 Nov live map will confirm): 389 and 589 at Hořešovice (8 of 9, modelled as one Simutrans line), 324 Smečno shorts on as 627 to Slaný (8 of 10), 617 on as 620 at Kladno (9 of 15; as one circuit 617 + 620 would need 8 buses instead of 9), 622 on as 617 at Kladno (6 of 8), 650 on as 618 at Kladno (10 of 15), 618 and 630 at Kladno (12 of 22), 300 on as 616 at Kladno, Sítná (3 of 3), 580 and 588 at Slaný (5 of 12), 597 on as 626 at Velvary (2 of 4), 628 on as 417 at Mšec (2 of 2); at Praha, Nádraží Veleslavín 300, 330 and 399 (LAMER / POHL / Arriva) swap buses (19 of the 45 departures of 399 follow a 300 arrival within 5 min, 19 a 399 arrival).

How to read the line cards

Typing the timetable. Values go into the Scheduling tab of the terminal entry, with the calendar on: Departure every, Offset, Also at (at most 7). Offsets count from 01:00 and every terminal is set as a loop, so each value is (real departure − 61) mod interval; the real clock minute is under every value. A row with Every 0 has no timetable: the bus leaves on arrival.

Core points and knots. Each line was read over the whole operating day (5:00–23:00) at its core points: terminals, knots where variants split, shared hubs. A trip counts at the time it passes a core point, wherever it starts. Where a slot is defined at a knot, the terminal departure is the knot time minus the running time, and the knot gets no timetable.

Variants. Each destination, path or shift variant is its own Simutrans line on a longer interval (the save game's Ex4). Shift lines repeat every 480 (8 h), the longest interval allowed. Trips more than 3 min off every slot are off the pattern, in orange.

Buses needed. One bus followed round the line's own slots with the real running times and at least 3 min at each terminal. Real buses swap lines at the terminals, so per-line totals are higher than what the operators run; pooling lines is the owner's choice and is not done.

In the game. Each card compares the save's line (export of 30 Oct 2026) with the modelled stops. The save's buses are shown as a check; the sheet is built from the real timetable and the buses seen running, so a game line may need buses added or moved.

To check

Choices made here that you may want to flip, each with its alternative; then the Simutrans lines whose model window has a slot without a real departure, or a real departure more than 3 min off.

How this was built

Scope. PID GTFS downloaded Sun 1 Nov 2026 (feed 1-14 Nov). Service day Mon 2 Nov 2026 (the first live-map poll day), a normal school weekday (28 Oct was a holiday and 29-30 Oct autumn school holidays; regional lines run many school-day-only trips). The Středočeský kraj licenses each district's lines with its own prefix (22xxxx = okres Kladno); a line licensed by Prague or by another kraj is on the sheet of the okres of its outer end (the first stop, coming in from the far end, in a Středočeský municipality), or, with no Prague end, of the okres most of its stops lie in. 47 lines: 45 modelled here, 0 on the Prague bus sheet, 2 listed with the reason.

Method. pipeline/bus-schedule-method.md (v4): whole-day reading at core points, repeating interval, Ex4 variants, timing at terminals worked back from the knots, no daily lines (at most every 480), every afternoon stop on a line, buses from the pattern, every line checked by hand (method-check file in the pipeline folder).

Buses. PID live map (mapa.pid.cz getData.php), polled every minute 12:00-20:00 (every 3 min 5:00-12:00 and 20:00-23:00) on Mon 2 Nov 2026, and on Tue 3 Nov 2026 so far only 5:00-7:20 (this version, 3 Nov morning: the Tuesday trips fill only designed buses and missing types; the evening version adds the whole Tuesday), by scripts/01_live_poll.py; each bus once per line through getVehicleWindow.php (operator, type, course). A bus fills one Simutrans line it ran in that line's window, by matching, across all twelve oblast sheets; buses on the Prague bus sheet are not used here.

Sources: PID GTFS, PID live map, the save-game export of 30 Oct 2026 (engine session).
